  \proofstepwidestar[]{\powerset(D\cup\{k\})
    \subseteq\mathcal O\cup(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq\forall x(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{%
      \rUI{\rRI{5,21}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\powerset(D\cup\{k\})
    =\mathcal O\cup(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep B\subseteq A\vdash A=B}[thm]{22,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cap\mathcal C=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\setminus C\vdash A\cap C=\varnothing}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{K\in\mathcal O\cap\mathcal E}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{K\in\mathcal O\land K\in\mathcal E}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\cap B\eqvdash x\in A\land x\in B}[thm]{25}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{K\in\powerset(D)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{3,\rAEa{26}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{K\subseteq D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{27}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{\{k\}\subseteq K}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{CoreFamilyMembership}[thm]{%
      \rAEb{26},\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaReserveFamiliesDef}[def]}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{k\in K}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
      29,\FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a\}}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{k\in D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{28,30}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{k\notin D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaKNotInD}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaKOutsideLayers}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[25]{\bot}{\rBI{32,31}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cap\mathcal E=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,x\notin A\vdash A=\varnothing}[thm]{%
      \rUI{\rCI{25,33}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cap(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\cap(B\cup C)=(A\cap B)\cup(A\cap C)}[thm]{24,34}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \powerset(D\cup\{k\})=\mathcal O\cup(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)
    \dsep\mathcal O\cap(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)=\varnothing}{%
    \rAI{23,35}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die lokale Potenzmengenabbildung ist bijektiv und nulltreu]{%
  \eta\colon\powerset(D)\bij\powerset(D\cup\{k\})\dsep
  \eta(\varnothing)=\varnothing%
}{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{D\dsep k\dsep\mathcal O\dsep\mathcal C\dsep\mathcal E}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\psi\dsep\eta}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cap\mathcal C=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\setminus C\vdash A\cap C=\varnothing}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cap(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaPartition}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Id_{\mathcal O}\colon\mathcal O\bij\mathcal O}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\Id_A\colon A\bij A}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\psi\colon\mathcal C\bij\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPsiBijective}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\CaseFun{\mathcal O}{\mathcal C}{\Id_{\mathcal O}}{\psi}
    \colon\mathcal O\cup\mathcal C\bij
      \mathcal O\cup(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{CaseFunBijectiveDisjointTargets}[thm]{1,2,3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cup\mathcal C=\powerset(D)}{%
    \rChain{%
      \rLRS{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def]},
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\cup B=B\cup A}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{UnionWithRelativeRemainder}[thm]{%
          \rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{%
            \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaReserveFamilyFacts}[thm]}}}}}}}}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathcal O\cup(\mathcal C\cup\mathcal E)
    =\powerset(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{%
      \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaPartition}[thm]}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta\colon\powerset(D)\bij\powerset(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def],5,6,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\varnothing\in\powerset(D)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\subseteq A}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{P\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in\{a,b\}}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDPrimeDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\varnothing\notin\mathcal C}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{CoreFamilyMembership}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaReserveFamiliesDef}[def],10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\varnothing\in\mathcal O}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]{%
      \rAI{9,11},\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta(\varnothing)=\Id_{\mathcal O}(\varnothing)}{%
    \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{f\colon A\to C\dsep g\colon B\to C\dsep x\in A
        \vdash\CaseFun{A}{B}{f}{g}(x)=f(x)}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep B\subseteq C
            \vdash F\colon A\to C}[thm]{%
              \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{3},
              \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq A\cup B}[thm]},
          \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep B\subseteq C
            \vdash F\colon A\to C}[thm]{%
              \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{4},
              \FormulaRefAuto{B\subseteq A\cup B}[thm]},12}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Id_{\mathcal O}(\varnothing)=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]{12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta(\varnothing)=\varnothing}{\rChain{13,14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta\colon\powerset(D)\bij\powerset(D\cup\{k\})
    \dsep\eta(\varnothing)=\varnothing}{\rAI{8,15}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Außerhalb der Reserve wirkt die lokale Abbildung identisch]{%
  K\in\powerset(D)\dsep K\notin\mathcal C\vdash\eta(K)=K%
}{MogiljanskajaEtaFixesOutsideReserve}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{D\dsep\mathcal O\dsep\mathcal C\dsep\mathcal E\dsep K}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\psi\dsep\eta}
}
\begin{tabproof}
  \proofstep{1}{K\in\powerset(D)}{\rA}
  \proofstep{2}{K\notin\mathcal C}{\rA}
  \proofstep{1,2}{K\in\mathcal O}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]{%
      \rAI{1,2},\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def]}}
  \proofstep{1,2}{\eta(K)=\Id_{\mathcal O}(K)}{%
    \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaDef}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{f\colon A\to C\dsep g\colon B\to C\dsep x\in A
        \vdash\CaseFun{A}{B}{f}{g}(x)=f(x)}[thm]{%
          \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep B\subseteq C
            \vdash F\colon A\to C}[thm]{%
              \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{%
                \FormulaRefAuto{\Id_A\colon A\bij A}[thm]},
              \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq A\cup B}[thm]},
          \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep B\subseteq C
            \vdash F\colon A\to C}[thm]{%
              \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{%
                \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPsiBijective}[thm]},
              \FormulaRefAuto{B\subseteq A\cup B}[thm]},3}}}
  \proofstep{1,2}{\Id_{\mathcal O}(K)=K}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]{3}}
  \proofstep{1,2}{\eta(K)=K}{\rChain{4,5}}
\end{tabproof}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Geschützte Schicht und globale Abbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    A_*&\coloneqq L\cup\{\mathbf0\},\\[-2pt]
    \Phi&\coloneqq
      \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}
      \restriction_{\PowNE{S}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A_*\dsep L\dsep D\dsep S\dsep T\dsep\mathbf0\dsep k}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\eta\dsep\Phi}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Trägerzerlegung für die Schichtfortsetzung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &A_*\cap D=\varnothing\dsep A_*\cap(D\cup\{k\})=\varnothing,\\[-2pt]
    &S=A_*\cup D\dsep T=A_*\cup(D\cup\{k\})
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A_*\dsep L\dsep D\dsep S\dsep T\dsep\mathbf0\dsep k}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{L\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayersDisjoint}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathbf0\notin D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaZeroNotInD}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaZeroOutsideLayers}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\{\mathbf0\}\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{SingletonDisjointFromAvoidedSet}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cup B)\cap C=(A\cap C)\cup(B\cap C)}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def],1,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{k\notin A_*}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaKOutsideCarrierPieces}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap\{k\}=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{AvoidedSingletonDisjoint}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap(D\cup\{k\})=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\cap(B\cup C)=(A\cap B)\cup(A\cap C)}[thm]{4,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{S=A_*\cup D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaCarrierSDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\cup(B\cup C)=(A\cup B)\cup C}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{T=A_*\cup(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaCarrierTDef}[def]{8,
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\cup(B\cup C)=(A\cup B)\cup C}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    A_*\cap D=\varnothing\dsep A_*\cap(D\cup\{k\})=\varnothing\dsep
    S=A_*\cup D\dsep T=A_*\cup(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \rAI{4,\rAI{7,\rAI{8,9}}}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die globale Abbildung ist bijektiv]{%
  \Phi\colon\PowNE{S}\bij\PowNE{T}%
}{MogiljanskajaPhiBijective}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A_*\dsep D\dsep S\dsep T\dsep k}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\eta\dsep\Phi}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap(D\cup\{k\})=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{S=A_*\cup D}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{T=A_*\cup(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta\colon\powerset(D)\bij\powerset(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta(\varnothing)=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}
      \restriction_{\PowNE{A_*\cup D}}
    \colon\PowNE{A_*\cup D}\bij\PowNE{A_*\cup(D\cup\{k\})}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LayerPowerMapNonemptyBijective}[thm]{1,2,5,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Phi\colon\PowNE{S}\bij\PowNE{T}}{%
    \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def],3,4,7}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Grundgleichung und Invarianten der globalen Abbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &X\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
      \Phi(X)=(X\cap A_*)\cup\eta(X\cap D),\\[-2pt]
    &X\in\PowNE{S}\vdash\Phi(X)\cap L=X\cap L,\\[-2pt]
    &X\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
      \bigl(X\subseteq L\leftrightarrow\Phi(X)\subseteq L\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaPhiInvariants}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A_*\dsep L\dsep D\dsep S\dsep T\dsep k\dsep X}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\eta\dsep\Phi}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\powerset(S)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\setminus B\subseteq A}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{S=A_*\cup D}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\powerset(A_*\cup D)}{\rIE{3,2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta\colon\powerset(D)\to\powerset(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{%
      \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Phi(X)=
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}(X)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\dsep x\in C\vdash F\restriction_C(x)=F(x)}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def],
      \rIE{3,\FormulaRefAuto{A\setminus B\subseteq A}[thm]},1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}(X)
    =(X\cap A_*)\cup\eta(X\cap D)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LayerPowerMapValue}[thm]{5,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Phi(X)=(X\cap A_*)\cup\eta(X\cap D)}{%
    \rChain{6,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap(D\cup\{k\})=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}(X)\cap A_*=X\cap A_*}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LayerPowerMapStablePart}[thm]{9,5,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{L\subseteq A_*}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq A\cup B}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Phi(X)\cap L=X\cap L}{%
    \rChain{%
      \rLRS{6},
      \rLRS{\FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{B\subseteq A\eqvdash A\cap B=B}[thm]{11}}},
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cap B)\cap C=A\cap(B\cap C)}[thm]},
      \rLRS{10},
      \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cap B)\cap C=A\cap(B\cap C)}[thm],
      \rLRS{\FormulaRefAuto{B\subseteq A\eqvdash A\cap B=B}[thm]{11}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{A_*\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta\colon\powerset(D)\bij\powerset(D\cup\{k\})}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\eta(\varnothing)=\varnothing}{%
    \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\subseteq L\leftrightarrow
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}(X)\subseteq L}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LayerPowerMapPreservesSubsets}[thm]{%
      13,9,14,15,11,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\subseteq L\leftrightarrow\Phi(X)\subseteq L}{%
    \rIE{6,16}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\Phi(X)=(X\cap A_*)\cup\eta(X\cap D),\\[-2pt]
      &\Phi(X)\cap L=X\cap L,\\[-2pt]
      &X\subseteq L\leftrightarrow\Phi(X)\subseteq L
    \end{aligned}}{\rAI{8,\rAI{12,17}}}
\end{tabproofwide}


\FormulaDefDeltaK[Rechteck- und Nullanteil eines Mengenprodukts]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    R(X,Y)&\coloneqq\bigl\{z\in D\bigm|
      \exists i,j\in\mathbb N\,
      (z=b_{ij}\land a_i\in X\cap L\land a_j\in Y\cap L)\bigr\},\\[-2pt]
    N_0(X,Y)&\coloneqq
      \IfThenElse{X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L}
        {\varnothing}{\{\mathbf0\}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{D\dsep L\dsep\mathbf0\dsep X\dsep Y\dsep z
    \dsep a_i\dsep a_j\dsep b_{ij}\dsep i\dsep j}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium des Nullanteils]{%
  z\in N_0(X,Y)\leftrightarrow
  \bigl(z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)\bigr)%
}{MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipNormalForm}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{L\dsep\mathbf0\dsep X\dsep Y\dsep z\dsep N_0(X,Y)}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Hinrichtung]{%
    z\in N_0(X,Y)\vdash
    z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)
  }{%
    z\in N_0(X,Y)\vdash
    z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)
  }[MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipForward]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{z\in N_0(X,Y)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      (X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)\lor
      \neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{P\lor\neg P}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{N_0(X,Y)=\varnothing}{%
      \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def],
        \FormulaRefAuto{P\vdash\IfThenElse{P}{a}{b}=a}[thm]{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{z\in\varnothing}{\rIE{4,1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{z\notin\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x \not\in \varnothing}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{\bot}{\rBI{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{P\dsep\neg P\vdash Q}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[9]{\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[9]{N_0(X,Y)=\{\mathbf0\}}{%
      \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def],
        \FormulaRefAuto{\neg P\vdash\IfThenElse{P}{a}{b}=b}[thm]{9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,9]{z\in\{\mathbf0\}}{\rIE{10,1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,9]{z=\mathbf0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,9]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{%
      \rAI{12,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{%
      \rOE{2,3,8,9,13}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Rückrichtung]{%
    z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)
    \vdash z\in N_0(X,Y)
  }{%
    z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)
    \vdash z\in N_0(X,Y)
  }[MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipBackward]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{z=\mathbf0}{\rAEa{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{\rAEb{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{N_0(X,Y)=\{\mathbf0\}}{%
      \rIE{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def],
        \FormulaRefAuto{\neg P\vdash\IfThenElse{P}{a}{b}=b}[thm]{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{z\in\{\mathbf0\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{z\in N_0(X,Y)}{\rIE{4,5}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{z\in N_0(X,Y)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipForward}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{z\in N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipBackward}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{z\in N_0(X,Y)\leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z=\mathbf0\land
      \neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L)\bigr)}{%
      \rBI{\rRI{1,2},\rRI{3,4}}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Punktweise Fälle der zweiten Multiplikation]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &i,j\in\mathbb N\vdash a_i\MogDiamond a_j=b_{ij},\\[-2pt]

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementnormalform des ersten Mengenprodukts]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &z\in X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y\leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z\in R(X,Y)\lor
      (z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L))\bigr)

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Gemeinsame Normalform beider Mengenprodukte]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
      X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y),\\[-2pt]
    &X,Y\in\PowNE{T}\vdash
      X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaPowerProductFormulas}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{S\dsep T\dsep L\dsep D\dsep\mathbf0\dsep k\dsep
    X\dsep Y\dsep z\dsep x\dsep y\dsep i\dsep j}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\MogStar\dsep\MogDiamond\dsep
    \MogStar_{\mathcal P}\dsep\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Erstes Mengenprodukt]{%
    X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
    X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
  }{%
    X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
    X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
  }[MogiljanskajaStarPowerProductFormula]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{z\in X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
      \leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z\in R(X,Y)\lor
        (z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L))\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaStarPowerMembershipNormalForm}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{z\in R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
      \leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z\in R(X,Y)\lor
        (z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L))\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\cup B\eqvdash x\in A\lor x\in B}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipNormalForm}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{z\in X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
      \leftrightarrow z\in R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{P\leftrightarrow R\dsep Q\leftrightarrow R
        \vdash P\leftrightarrow Q}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
      =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall z\,(z\in A\leftrightarrow z\in B)\vdash A=B}[ax]{%
        \rUI{5}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Zweites Mengenprodukt]{%
    X,Y\in\PowNE{T}\vdash
    X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
  }{%
    X,Y\in\PowNE{T}\vdash
    X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
  }[MogiljanskajaDiamondPowerProductFormula]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\PowNE{T}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\in\PowNE{T}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{z\in X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y
      \leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z\in R(X,Y)\lor
        (z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L))\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDiamondPowerMembershipNormalForm}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{z\in R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
      \leftrightarrow
      \bigl(z\in R(X,Y)\lor
        (z=\mathbf0\land\neg(X\subseteq L\land Y\subseteq L))\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\cup B\eqvdash x\in A\lor x\in B}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaNullPartMembershipNormalForm}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{z\in X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y
      \leftrightarrow z\in R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{P\leftrightarrow R\dsep Q\leftrightarrow R
        \vdash P\leftrightarrow Q}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y
      =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall z\,(z\in A\leftrightarrow z\in B)\vdash A=B}[ax]{%
        \rUI{5}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{X,Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
      =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaStarPowerProductFormula}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{X,Y\in\PowNE{T}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y
      =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDiamondPowerProductFormula}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y),\\[-2pt]
        &X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y=R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)
      \end{aligned}}{\rAI{2,4}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Kein Produktrechteck liegt in der Reservefamilie]{%
  X,Y\in\powerset(T)\vdash R(X,Y)\notin\mathcal C%
}{MogiljanskajaRectangleOutsideReserve}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{T\dsep D\dsep D'\dsep P\dsep V\dsep L\dsep
    \mathcal C\dsep X\dsep Y\dsep c_0\dsep c_1\dsep b_{01}\dsep R(X,Y)}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\powerset(T)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\in\powerset(T)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{R(X,Y)\in\mathcal C}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{P\subseteq R(X,Y)\land R(X,Y)\subseteq P\cup V}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{CoreFamilyMembership}[thm]{3,
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaReserveFamiliesDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{c_0,c_1\in P}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a,b\}\eqvdash(x=a\lor x=b)}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDPrimeDef}[def]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{c_0,c_1\in R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
      \rAEa{4},5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{a_0\in X\cap L\land a_1\in Y\cap L}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]{6,
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDPrimeDef}[def],
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaBIndexUnique}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{b_{01}\in R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax],\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOneInN}[thm],7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{P\cup V=D'}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaDPrimeDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{R(X,Y)\subseteq D'}{\rIE{9,\rAEb{4}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{b_{01}\in D'}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{10,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{b_{01}\notin D'}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{\rAEb{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaReserveMarkerFacts}[thm]}}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{\bot}{\rBI{12,11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R(X,Y)\notin\mathcal C}{\rCI{3,13}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Der Produktschichtanteil ist genau das Rechteck]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
      (X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y),\\[-2pt]
    &X,Y\in\PowNE{T}\vdash
      (X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)
  \end{aligned}%
}{MogiljanskajaPowerProductDParts}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{S\dsep T\dsep D\dsep L\dsep\mathbf0\dsep
    X\dsep Y\dsep R(X,Y)\dsep N_0(X,Y)}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\MogStar_{\mathcal P}\dsep\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{R(X,Y)\subseteq D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{R(X,Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\eqvdash A\cap B=A}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\mathbf0\notin D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaZeroNotInD}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaZeroOutsideLayers}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{N_0(X,Y)\cap D=\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]{3,
      \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\cap A=\varnothing}[thm],
      \FormulaRefAuto{SingletonDisjointFromAvoidedSet}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{X,Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
    =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerProductFormulas}[thm]{5}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cup B)\cap C=(A\cap C)\cup(B\cap C)}[thm]{%
      6,2,4,\FormulaRefAuto{A\cup\varnothing=A}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{X,Y\in\PowNE{T}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y
    =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
    \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerProductFormulas}[thm]{8}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{(X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cup B)\cap C=(A\cap C)\cup(B\cap C)}[thm]{%
      9,2,4,\FormulaRefAuto{A\cup\varnothing=A}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5,8]{%
    (X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)\dsep
    (X\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)}{\rAI{7,10}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die globale Abbildung fixiert jedes erste Mengenprodukt]{%
  X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
  \Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)=X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y%
}{MogiljanskajaPhiFixesStarProduct}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A_*\dsep D\dsep S\dsep T\dsep X\dsep Y\dsep R(X,Y)}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\eta\dsep\Phi\dsep\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R(X,Y)\subseteq D}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R(X,Y)\in\powerset(D)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R(X,Y)\notin\mathcal C}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaRectangleOutsideReserve}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep B\subseteq C\vdash A\subseteq C}[thm]{%
          \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyPowersetMembership}[thm]{1}},
          \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaSSubsetT}[thm]}},
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep B\subseteq C\vdash A\subseteq C}[thm]{%
          \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyPowersetMembership}[thm]{2}},
          \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaSSubsetT}[thm]}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\eta(R(X,Y))=R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaFixesOutsideReserve}[thm]{4,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D=R(X,Y)}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerProductDParts}[thm]{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\eta((X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D)
    =(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)\cap D}{\rIE{7,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{S}{\MogStar}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaFirstSemigroup}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y\in\PowNE{S}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PowerSemigroupProductClosure}[thm]{9,1,2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y\in\powerset(A_*\cup D)}{%
    \rIE{\rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}},
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\setminus B\subseteq A}[thm]{10}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}
      (X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)=X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LayerPowerMapFixesStableUnion}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{%
        \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaEtaBijective}[thm]}},11,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)=
    \LayerPowerMap{A_*}{D}{D\cup\{k\}}{\eta}
      (X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\dsep x\in C\vdash F\restriction_C(x)=F(x)}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiDef}[def],
      \rIE{\rAEa{\rAEb{\rAEb{%
        \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaLayerCarrierFacts}[thm]}}},
        \FormulaRefAuto{A\setminus B\subseteq A}[thm]},10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)
    =X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y}{\rChain{13,12}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die globale Bijektion erhält das Mengenprodukt]{%
  X,Y\in\PowNE{S}\vdash
  \Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)
  =\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)%
}{MogiljanskajaPhiPreservesPowerProduct}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{S\dsep T\dsep L\dsep X\dsep Y}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\Phi\dsep\MogStar_{\mathcal P}\dsep
    \MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Phi(X)\cap L=X\cap L}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiInvariants}[thm]{1}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{\Phi(Y)\cap L=Y\cap L}{%
    \rAEa{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiInvariants}[thm]{2}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{X\subseteq L\leftrightarrow\Phi(X)\subseteq L}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiInvariants}[thm]{1}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{Y\subseteq L\leftrightarrow\Phi(Y)\subseteq L}{%
    \rAEb{\rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiInvariants}[thm]{2}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{R(\Phi(X),\Phi(Y))=R(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{N_0(\Phi(X),\Phi(Y))=N_0(X,Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaProductPartsDef}[def]{5,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Phi\colon\PowNE{S}\to\PowNE{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiBijective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Phi(X)\in\PowNE{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{9,1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{\Phi(Y)\in\PowNE{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{9,2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)
    =R(\Phi(X),\Phi(Y))\cup N_0(\Phi(X),\Phi(Y))}{%
    \rAEb{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerProductFormulas}[thm]{10,11}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)
    =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{\rChain{12,\rLRS{7},\rLRS{8}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
    =R(X,Y)\cup N_0(X,Y)}{%
    \rAEa{\FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerProductFormulas}[thm]{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y
    =\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)}{%
    \rChain{14,\FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{13}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)
    =X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiFixesStarProduct}[thm]{1,2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)
    =\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)}{\rChain{16,15}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die konstruierten Potenzhalbgruppen sind isomorph]{%
  \SemigroupIso{\Phi}
    {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
    {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}%
}{MogiljanskajaPowerSemigroupIsomorphism}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{S\dsep T\dsep X\dsep Y}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\Phi\dsep\MogStar\dsep\MogDiamond
    \dsep\MogStar_{\mathcal P}\dsep\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{S}{\MogStar}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaFirstSemigroup}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{T}{\MogDiamond}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaSecondSemigroup}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{\PowNE{S}}{\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyPowerSemigroup}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{\PowNE{T}}{\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyPowerSemigroup}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Phi\colon\PowNE{S}\bij\PowNE{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiBijective}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Phi\colon\PowNE{S}\to\PowNE{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Bijektive Funktion}[def]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[7]{X\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{Y\in\PowNE{S}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[7,8]{\Phi(X\MogStar_{\mathcal P}Y)
    =\Phi(X)\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}\Phi(Y)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPhiPreservesPowerProduct}[thm]{7,8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupHom{\Phi}
    {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
    {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{SemigroupHomDef}[def]{3,4,6,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupIso{\Phi}
    {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
    {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{SemigroupIsoDef}[def]{10,5}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Das Mogiljanskaja-Paar ist ein unendliches
Gegenbeispiel]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\SemigroupAlg{S}{\MogStar}\land
      \SemigroupAlg{T}{\MogDiamond}\land
      \neg\Finite{S}\land\neg\Finite{T}\land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\neg\exists f\,\SemigroupIso{f}{S,\MogStar}{T,\MogDiamond}\land
      \exists F\,\SemigroupIso{F}
        {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
        {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
  \end{aligned}
}{MogiljanskajaPairCounterexample}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep F\dsep\Phi\dsep
    \MogStar\dsep\MogDiamond\dsep
    \MogStar_{\mathcal P}\dsep\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{S}{\MogStar}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaFirstSemigroup}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupAlg{T}{\MogDiamond}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaSecondSemigroup}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\neg\Finite{S}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaCarrierSInfinite}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\neg\Finite{T}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaCarrierTInfinite}[thm]{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaBetaInjective}[thm]}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\neg\exists f\,
    \SemigroupIso{f}{S,\MogStar}{T,\MogDiamond}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaNotIsomorphic}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\SemigroupIso{\Phi}
    {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
    {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{MogiljanskajaPowerSemigroupIsomorphism}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\exists F\,\SemigroupIso{F}
    {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
    {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}}{\rEI{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\SemigroupAlg{S}{\MogStar}\land
        \SemigroupAlg{T}{\MogDiamond}\land
        \neg\Finite{S}\land\neg\Finite{T}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\neg\exists f\,\SemigroupIso{f}{S,\MogStar}{T,\MogDiamond}\land
        \exists F\,\SemigroupIso{F}
          {\PowNE{S},\MogStar_{\mathcal P}}
          {\PowNE{T},\MogDiamond_{\mathcal P}}
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \rAI{1,\rAI{2,\rAI{3,\rAI{4,\rAI{5,7}}}}}}
\end{tabproofwide}

Damit ist das Mogiljanskaja-Paar ein Gegenbeispiel zur Umkehrung von
\FormulaRefAuto{SemigroupIsoImpliesPowerSemigroupIso}[thm].
Die Umkehrung ist also ohne Endlichkeitsvoraussetzung falsch.
